/*
 * ===================================================================
 * AD8232 ECG SENSOR HEADER FILE
 * ===================================================================
 */

#ifndef AD8232_H
#define AD8232_H

#include <Arduino.h>

// ==========================================
// HÃ€M KHá»žI Táº O VÃ€ Cáº¬P NHáº¬T
// ==========================================

/**
 * Khá»Ÿi táº¡o AD8232 vá»›i ADS1115
 * Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh I2C, ADC, vÃ  cÃ¡c bá»™ lá»c
 */
void initAD8232();

/**
 * Cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘á»c tÃ­n hiá»‡u ECG vÃ  phÃ¡t hiá»‡n nhá»‹p tim
 * Gá»i hÃ m nÃ y trong loop() chÃ­nh
 */
void updateAD8232();

// ==========================================
// HÃ€M Láº¤Y Dá»® LIá»†U
// ==========================================

/**
 * Láº¥y tÃ­n hiá»‡u ECG thÃ´ (chÆ°a lá»c) tá»« ADS1115
 * @return GiÃ¡ trá»‹ Ä‘iá»‡n Ã¡p (mV)
 */
float getECGRawSignal();

/**
 * Láº¥y tÃ­n hiá»‡u ECG Ä‘Ã£ qua bá»™ lá»c nhiá»…u
 * @return GiÃ¡ trá»‹ Ä‘iá»‡n Ã¡p Ä‘Ã£ lá»c (mV)
 */
float getECGFilteredSignal();

/**
 * Láº¥y nhá»‹p tim hiá»‡n táº¡i
 * @return Nhá»‹p tim (BPM - beats per minute), 0 náº¿u khÃ´ng phÃ¡t hiá»‡n
 */
int getHeartRate();

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra tráº¡ng thÃ¡i káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘iá»‡n cá»±c
 * @return true náº¿u Ä‘iá»‡n cá»±c Ä‘Æ°á»£c gáº¯n Ä‘Ãºng, false náº¿u bá»‹ tuá»™t
 */
bool areLeadsConnected();

// ==========================================
// HÃ€M DEBUG (TÃ™Y CHá»ŒN)
// ==========================================

/**
 * In thÃ´ng tin debug ra Serial Monitor
 * Bao gá»“m: tráº¡ng thÃ¡i leads, raw signal, filtered signal, heart rate
 */
void printECGDebug();

#endif // AD8232_H

/**
 * @file lv_conf.h
 * Configuration file for v8.4.0
 */

/*
 * Copy this file as `lv_conf.h`
 * 1. simply next to the `lvgl` folder
 * 2. or any other places and
 *    - define `LV_CONF_INCLUDE_SIMPLE`
 *    - add the path as include path
 */

/* clang-format off */
#if 1 /*Set it to "1" to enable content*/

#ifndef LV_CONF_H
#define LV_CONF_H

#include <stdint.h>

/*====================
   COLOR SETTINGS
 *====================*/

/*Color depth: 1 (1 byte per pixel), 8 (RGB332), 16 (RGB565), 32 (ARGB8888)*/
#define LV_COLOR_DEPTH 16

/*Swap the 2 bytes of RGB565 color. Useful if the display has an 8-bit interface (e.g. SPI)*/
#define LV_COLOR_16_SWAP 0

/*Enable features to draw on transparent background.
 *It's required if opa, and transform_* style properties are used.
 *Can be also used if the UI is above another layer, e.g. an OSD menu or video player.*/
#define LV_COLOR_SCREEN_TRANSP 0

/* Adjust color mix functions rounding. GPUs might calculate color mix (blending) differently.
 * 0: round down, 64: round up from x.75, 128: round up from half, 192: round up from x.25, 254: round up */
#define LV_COLOR_MIX_ROUND_OFS 0

/*Images pixels with this color will not be drawn if they are chroma keyed)*/
#define LV_COLOR_CHROMA_KEY lv_color_hex(0x00ff00)         /*pure green*/

/*=========================
   MEMORY SETTINGS
 *=========================*/

/*1: use custom malloc/free, 0: use the built-in `LV_TICK_CUSTOM()` and `lv_mem_free()`*/
#define LV_MEM_CUSTOM 1
#if LV_MEM_CUSTOM == 0
    /*Size of the memory available for `lv_mem_alloc()` in bytes (>= 2kB)*/
    #define LV_MEM_SIZE (120U * 1024U)          /*[bytes]*/

    /*Set an address for the memory pool instead of allocating it as a normal array. Can be in external SRAM too.*/
    #define LV_MEM_ADR 0     /*0: unused*/
    /*Instead of an address give a memory allocator that will be called to get a memory pool for LVGL. E.g. my_malloc*/
    #if LV_MEM_ADR == 0
        #undef LV_MEM_POOL_INCLUDE
        #undef LV_MEM_POOL_ALLOC
    #endif

#else       /*LV_MEM_CUSTOM*/
    #define LV_MEM_CUSTOM_INCLUDE <stdlib.h>   /*Header for the dynamic memory function*/
    #define LV_MEM_CUSTOM_ALLOC   malloc
    #define LV_MEM_CUSTOM_FREE    free
    #define LV_MEM_CUSTOM_REALLOC realloc
#endif     /*LV_MEM_CUSTOM*/

/*Number of the intermediate memory buffer used during rendering and other internal processing mechanisms.
 *You will see an error log message if there wasn't enough buffers. */
#define LV_MEM_BUF_MAX_NUM 16

/*Use the standard `memcpy` and `memset` instead of LVGL's own functions. (Might or might not be faster).*/
#define LV_MEMCPY_MEMSET_STD 0

/*====================
   HAL SETTINGS
 *====================*/

/*Default display refresh period. LVG will redraw changed areas with this period time*/
#define LV_DISP_DEF_REFR_PERIOD 30      /*[ms]*/

/*Input device read period in milliseconds*/
#define LV_INDEV_DEF_READ_PERIOD 30     /*[ms]*/

/*Use a custom tick source that tells the elapsed time in milliseconds.
 *It removes the need to manually update the tick with `lv_tick_inc()`)*/
#define LV_TICK_CUSTOM 1
#if LV_TICK_CUSTOM
    #define LV_TICK_CUSTOM_INCLUDE "Arduino.h"         /*Header for the system time function*/
    #define LV_TICK_CUSTOM_SYS_TIME_EXPR (millis())    /*Expression evaluating to current system time in ms*/
    /*If using lvgl as ESP32 component*/
    // #define LV_TICK_CUSTOM_INCLUDE "esp_timer.h"
    // #define LV_TICK_CUSTOM_SYS_TIME_EXPR ((esp_timer_get_time() / 1000LL))
#endif   /*LV_TICK_CUSTOM*/

/*Default Dot Per Inch. Used to initialize default sizes such as widgets sized, style paddings.
 *(Not so important, you can adjust it to modify default sizes and spaces)*/
#define LV_DPI_DEF 130     /*[px/inch]*/

/*=======================
 * FEATURE CONFIGURATION
 *=======================*/

/*-------------
 * Drawing
 *-----------*/

/*Enable complex draw engine.
 *Required to draw shadow, gradient, rounded corners, circles, arc, skew lines, image transformations or any masks*/
#define LV_DRAW_COMPLEX 1
#if LV_DRAW_COMPLEX != 0

    /*Allow buffering some shadow calculation.
    *LV_SHADOW_CACHE_SIZE is the max. shadow size to buffer, where shadow size is `shadow_width + radius`
    *Caching has LV_SHADOW_CACHE_SIZE^2 RAM cost*/
    #define LV_SHADOW_CACHE_SIZE 0

    /* Set number of maximally cached circle data.
    * The circumference of 1/4 circle are saved for anti-aliasing
    * radius * 4 bytes are used per circle (the most often used radiuses are saved)
    * 0: to disable caching */
    #define LV_CIRCLE_CACHE_SIZE 4
#endif /*LV_DRAW_COMPLEX*/

/**
 * "Simple layers" are used when a widget has `style_opa < 255` to buffer the widget into a layer
 * and blend it as an image with the given opacity.
 * Note that `bg_opa`, `text_opa` etc don't require buffering into layer)
 * The widget can be buffered in smaller chunks to avoid using large buffers.
 *
 * - LV_LAYER_SIMPLE_BUF_SIZE: [bytes] the optimal target buffer size. LVGL will try to allocate it
 * - LV_LAYER_SIMPLE_FALLBACK_BUF_SIZE: [bytes]  used if `LV_LAYER_SIMPLE_BUF_SIZE` couldn't be allocated.
 *
 * Both buffer sizes are in bytes.
 * "Transformed layers" (where transform_angle/zoom properties are used) use larger buffers
 * and can't be drawn in chunks. So these settings affects only widgets with opacity.
 */
#define LV_LAYER_SIMPLE_BUF_SIZE          (24 * 1024)
#define LV_LAYER_SIMPLE_FALLBACK_BUF_SIZE (3 * 1024)

/*Default image cache size. Image caching keeps the images opened.
 *If only the built-in image formats are used there is no real advantage of caching. (I.e. if no new image decoder is added)
 *With complex image decoders (e.g. PNG or JPG) caching can save the continuous open/decode of images.
 *However the opened images might consume additional RAM.
 *0: to disable caching*/
#define LV_IMG_CACHE_DEF_SIZE 0

/*Number of stops allowed per gradient. Increase this to allow more stops.
 *This adds (sizeof(lv_color_t) + 1) bytes per additional stop*/
#define LV_GRADIENT_MAX_STOPS 2

/*Default gradient buffer size.
 *When LVGL calculates the gradient "maps" it can save them into a cache to avoid calculating them again.
 *LV_GRAD_CACHE_DEF_SIZE sets the size of this cache in bytes.
 *If the cache is too small the map will be allocated only while it's required for the drawing.
 *0 mean no caching.*/
#define LV_GRAD_CACHE_DEF_SIZE 0

/*Allow dithering the gradients (to achieve visual smooth color gradients on limited color depth display)
 *LV_DITHER_GRADIENT implies allocating one or two more lines of the object's rendering surface
 *The increase in memory consumption is (32 bits * object width) plus 24 bits * object width if using error diffusion */
#define LV_DITHER_GRADIENT 0
#if LV_DITHER_GRADIENT
    /*Add support for error diffusion dithering.
     *Error diffusion dithering gets a much better visual result, but implies more CPU consumption and memory when drawing.
     *The increase in memory consumption is (24 bits * object's width)*/
    #define LV_DITHER_ERROR_DIFFUSION 0
#endif

/*Maximum buffer size to allocate for rotation.
 *Only used if software rotation is enabled in the display driver.*/
#define LV_DISP_ROT_MAX_BUF (10*1024)

/*-------------
 * GPU
 *-----------*/

/*Use Arm's 2D acceleration library Arm-2D */
#define LV_USE_GPU_ARM2D 0

/*Use STM32's DMA2D (aka Chrom Art) GPU*/
#define LV_USE_GPU_STM32_DMA2D 0
#if LV_USE_GPU_STM32_DMA2D
    /*Must be defined to include path of CMSIS header of target processor
    e.g. "stm32f7xx.h" or "stm32f4xx.h"*/
    #define LV_GPU_DMA2D_CMSIS_INCLUDE
#endif

/*Enable RA6M3 G2D GPU*/
#define LV_USE_GPU_RA6M3_G2D 0
#if LV_USE_GPU_RA6M3_G2D
    /*include path of target processor
    e.g. "hal_data.h"*/
    #define LV_GPU_RA6M3_G2D_INCLUDE "hal_data.h"
#endif

/*Use SWM341's DMA2D GPU*/
#define LV_USE_GPU_SWM341_DMA2D 0
#if LV_USE_GPU_SWM341_DMA2D
    #define LV_GPU_SWM341_DMA2D_INCLUDE "SWM341.h"
#endif

/*Use NXP's PXP GPU iMX RTxxx platforms*/
#define LV_USE_GPU_NXP_PXP 0
#if LV_USE_GPU_NXP_PXP
    /*1: Add default bare metal and FreeRTOS interrupt handling routines for PXP (lv_gpu_nxp_pxp_osa.c)
    *   and call lv_gpu_nxp_pxp_init() automatically during lv_init(). Note that symbol SDK_OS_FREE_RTOS
    *   has to be defined in order to use FreeRTOS OSA, otherwise bare-metal implementation is selected.
    *0: lv_gpu_nxp_pxp_init() has to be called manually before lv_init()
    */
    #define LV_USE_GPU_NXP_PXP_AUTO_INIT 0
#endif

/*Use NXP's VG-Lite GPU iMX RTxxx platforms*/
#define LV_USE_GPU_NXP_VG_LITE 0

/*Use SDL renderer API*/
#define LV_USE_GPU_SDL 0
#if LV_USE_GPU_SDL
    #define LV_GPU_SDL_INCLUDE_PATH <SDL2/SDL.h>
    /*Texture cache size, 8MB by default*/
    #define LV_GPU_SDL_LRU_SIZE (1024 * 1024 * 8)
    /*Custom blend mode for mask drawing, disable if you need to link with older SDL2 lib*/
    #define LV_GPU_SDL_CUSTOM_BLEND_MODE (SDL_VERSION_ATLEAST(2, 0, 6))
#endif

/*-------------
 * Logging
 *-----------*/

/*Enable the log module*/
#define LV_USE_LOG 0
#if LV_USE_LOG

    /*How important log should be added:
    *LV_LOG_LEVEL_TRACE       A lot of logs to give detailed information
    *LV_LOG_LEVEL_INFO        Log important events
    *LV_LOG_LEVEL_WARN        Log if something unwanted happened but didn't cause a problem
    *LV_LOG_LEVEL_ERROR       Only critical issue, when the system may fail
    *LV_LOG_LEVEL_USER        Only logs added by the user
    *LV_LOG_LEVEL_NONE        Do not log anything*/
    #define LV_LOG_LEVEL LV_LOG_LEVEL_WARN

    /*1: Print the log with 'printf';
    *0: User need to register a callback with `lv_log_register_print_cb()`*/
    #define LV_LOG_PRINTF 0

    /*Enable/disable LV_LOG_TRACE in modules that produces a huge number of logs*/
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_MEM        1
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_TIMER      1
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_INDEV      1
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_DISP_REFR  1
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_EVENT      1
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_OBJ_CREATE 1
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_LAYOUT     1
    #define LV_LOG_TRACE_ANIM       1

#endif  /*LV_USE_LOG*/

/*-------------
 * Asserts
 *-----------*/

/*Enable asserts if an operation is failed or an invalid data is found.
 *If LV_USE_LOG is enabled an error message will be printed on failure*/
#define LV_USE_ASSERT_NULL          1   /*Check if the parameter is NULL. (Very fast, recommended)*/
#define LV_USE_ASSERT_MALLOC        1   /*Checks is the memory is successfully allocated or no. (Very fast, recommended)*/
#define LV_USE_ASSERT_STYLE         0   /*Check if the styles are properly initialized. (Very fast, recommended)*/
#define LV_USE_ASSERT_MEM_INTEGRITY 0   /*Check the integrity of `lv_mem` after critical operations. (Slow)*/
#define LV_USE_ASSERT_OBJ           0   /*Check the object's type and existence (e.g. not deleted). (Slow)*/

/*Add a custom handler when assert happens e.g. to restart the MCU*/
#define LV_ASSERT_HANDLER_INCLUDE <stdint.h>
#define LV_ASSERT_HANDLER while(1);   /*Halt by default*/

/*-------------
 * Others
 *-----------*/

/*1: Show CPU usage and FPS count*/
#define LV_USE_PERF_MONITOR 0
#if LV_USE_PERF_MONITOR
    #define LV_USE_PERF_MONITOR_POS LV_ALIGN_BOTTOM_RIGHT
#endif

/*1: Show the used memory and the memory fragmentation
 * Requires LV_MEM_CUSTOM = 0*/
#define LV_USE_MEM_MONITOR 0
#if LV_USE_MEM_MONITOR
    #define LV_USE_MEM_MONITOR_POS LV_ALIGN_BOTTOM_LEFT
#endif

/*1: Draw random colored rectangles over the redrawn areas*/
#define LV_USE_REFR_DEBUG 0

/*Change the built in (v)snprintf functions*/
#define LV_SPRINTF_CUSTOM 0
#if LV_SPRINTF_CUSTOM
    #define LV_SPRINTF_INCLUDE <stdio.h>
    #define lv_snprintf  snprintf
    #define lv_vsnprintf vsnprintf
#else   /*LV_SPRINTF_CUSTOM*/
    #define LV_SPRINTF_USE_FLOAT 0
#endif  /*LV_SPRINTF_CUSTOM*/

#define LV_USE_USER_DATA 1

/*Garbage Collector settings
 *Used if lvgl is bound to higher level language and the memory is managed by that language*/
#define LV_ENABLE_GC 0
#if LV_ENABLE_GC != 0
    #define LV_GC_INCLUDE "gc.h"                           /*Include Garbage Collector related things*/
#endif /*LV_ENABLE_GC*/

/*=====================
 *  COMPILER SETTINGS
 *====================*/

/*For big endian systems set to 1*/
#define LV_BIG_ENDIAN_SYSTEM 0

/*Define a custom attribute to `lv_tick_inc` function*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_TICK_INC

/*Define a custom attribute to `lv_timer_handler` function*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_TIMER_HANDLER

/*Define a custom attribute to `lv_disp_flush_ready` function*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_FLUSH_READY

/*Required alignment size for buffers*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_MEM_ALIGN_SIZE 1

/*Will be added where memories needs to be aligned (with -Os data might not be aligned to boundary by default).
 * E.g. __attribute__((aligned(4)))*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_MEM_ALIGN

/*Attribute to mark large constant arrays for example font's bitmaps*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_LARGE_CONST

/*Compiler prefix for a big array declaration in RAM*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_LARGE_RAM_ARRAY

/*Place performance critical functions into a faster memory (e.g RAM)*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_FAST_MEM

/*Prefix variables that are used in GPU accelerated operations, often these need to be placed in RAM sections that are DMA accessible*/
#define LV_ATTRIBUTE_DMA

/*Export integer constant to binding. This macro is used with constants in the form of LV_<CONST> that
 *should also appear on LVGL binding API such as Micropython.*/
#define LV_EXPORT_CONST_INT(int_value) struct _silence_gcc_warning /*The default value just prevents GCC warning*/

/*Extend the default -32k..32k coordinate range to -4M..4M by using int32_t for coordinates instead of int16_t*/
#define LV_USE_LARGE_COORD 0

#ifndef UI_H
#define UI_H

#include <Arduino.h>
#include <lvgl.h>
#include <TFT_eSPI.h>

enum ScreenType
{
    SCR_BOOT,
    SCR_MENU,
    SCR_MONITOR, // MÃ n hÃ¬nh All-in-one (Giá»‘ng HTML)
    SCR_ECG,     // MÃ n hÃ¬nh ECG riÃªng
    SCR_SPO2,    // MÃ n hÃ¬nh HR/SpO2 riÃªng
    SCR_TEMP,    // MÃ n hÃ¬nh Temperature riÃªng
    SCR_WIFI_SCAN,
    SCR_WIFI_PASS,
    SCR_CONFIG // MÃ n hÃ¬nh Web Config (Giá»‘ng HTML)
};

// Biáº¿n toÃ n cá»¥c
extern ScreenType current_screen_type;

void ui_init();
void ui_switch_screen(ScreenType scr);
void ui_update_sensors(float temp, int hr, int spo2, int ecg_val);
void ui_input_read(lv_indev_drv_t *drv, lv_indev_data_t *data);

#endif

#ifndef UI_DATACOLLECTOR_H

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C"
{
#endif

    // ==========================================

    // ==========================================

    extern lv_obj_t *ui_IDValue;

    extern lv_obj_t *ui_ProgressPanel;

    extern lv_obj_t *ui_AmbientLabel;

    extern lv_obj_t *ui_InstructionLabel3;

    extern lv_obj_t *ui_PopupTitle;
    extern lv_obj_t *ui_PopupMessage;
    extern lv_obj_t *ui_PopupProgressBar;

    // ==========================================
    // PUBLIC FUNCTIONS
    // ==========================================
    /**
     * @brief Initialize all UI screens and components
     */
    void ui_datacollector_init(void);

    /**
     * @brief Update real-time sensor data on Monitor screen
     * @param distance Distance in mm
     * @param temperature Object temperature in Celsius
     * @param ambient Ambient temperature in Celsius
     */
    void ui_datacollector_update_sensors(float distance, float temperature, float ambient);

    /**
     * @brief Update ID value on Monitor screen
     * @param id Current person ID
     */
    void ui_datacollector_update_id(int id);

    /**
     * @brief Update progress counter
     * @param current Current sample count
     * @param total Total samples needed (default 5)
     */
    void ui_datacollector_update_progress(int current, int total);

    /**
     * @brief Update WiFi status icon
     * @param connected true if connected, false otherwise
     */
    void ui_datacollector_update_wifi(bool connected);

    /**
     * @brief Show popup with message
     * @param title Popup title
     * @param message Popup message
     * @param color Background color (e.g., lv_palette_main(LV_PALETTE_GREEN))
     * @param show_progress Show progress bar
     */
    void ui_datacollector_show_popup(const char *title, const char *message,
                                     lv_color_t color, bool show_progress);

    /**
     * @brief Hide popup
     */
    void ui_datacollector_hide_popup(void);

    /**
     * @brief Update popup progress bar (0-100)
     */
    void ui_datacollector_update_popup_progress(int percentage);

    /**
     * @brief Switch to Monitor screen
     */
    void ui_datacollector_load_monitor(void);

    // ==========================================
    // EVENT CALLBACKS (to be implemented in main.cpp)
    // ==========================================
    /**
     * @brief Called when ENTER button pressed (take measurement)
     */
    void ui_event_take_measurement(lv_event_t *e);

    /**
     * @brief Called when ID minus button pressed

    void ui_event_id_minus(lv_event_t *e);

    /**
     * @brief Called when ID plus button pressed
     */
    void ui_event_id_plus(lv_event_t *e);

    /**
     * @brief Called when sample reset button pressed
     */
    void ui_event_sample_reset(lv_event_t *e);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}

/*
 * ===================================================================
 * UI_ECG.H - ECG STANDALONE UI (KHÃ”NG áº¢NH HÆ¯á»žNG UI HIá»†N Táº I)
 * ===================================================================
 *
 * File UI riÃªng cho ECG monitoring, tÃ¡ch biá»‡t hoÃ n toÃ n vá»›i ui.h/ui.cpp
 *
 * FEATURES:
 * - Realtime ECG waveform chart (250Hz capable)
 * - Heart rate display with BPM detection
 * - Lead-off detection warning
 * - Professional medical-grade design
 * - Independent navigation (UP/DOWN/ENTER/BACK)
 *
 * USAGE:
 * 1. #include "ui_ecg.h" trong main.cpp
 * 2. ecg_ui_init() trong setup()
 * 3. ecg_ui_update(ecg_value) trong loop()
 */

#ifndef UI_ECG_H
#define UI_ECG_H

#include <Arduino.h>
#include <lvgl.h>
#include <TFT_eSPI.h>

// ==========================================
// PUBLIC API
// ==========================================

/**
 * Khá»Ÿi táº¡o ECG UI system
 * Gá»i 1 láº§n trong setup()
 *
 * @param tft_instance: Pointer tá»›i TFT_eSPI object
 */
void ecg_ui_init(TFT_eSPI *tft_instance);

/**
 * Update ECG waveform realtime
 * Gá»i trong loop() vá»›i táº§n sá»‘ 250Hz (má»—i 4ms)
 *
 * @param ecg_mV: ECG signal value in millivolts (-3.0 to +3.0)
 * @param hr_bpm: Heart rate in beats per minute (0-220)
 * @param leads_connected: true náº¿u Ä‘iá»‡n cá»±c Ä‘Æ°á»£c káº¿t ná»‘i
 */
void ecg_ui_update(float ecg_mV, int hr_bpm, bool leads_connected);

/**
 * LVGL task handler - gá»i trong loop()
 * Cáº§n gá»i Ã­t nháº¥t má»—i 5-10ms Ä‘á»ƒ UI mÆ°á»£t
 */
void ecg_ui_tick();

/**
 * Äá»c input tá»« buttons
 * Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c gá»i bá»Ÿi LVGL, khÃ´ng cáº§n gá»i thá»§ cÃ´ng
 */
void ecg_button_read(lv_indev_drv_t *drv, lv_indev_data_t *data);

/**
 * Reset chart vá» tráº¡ng thÃ¡i ban Ä‘áº§u
 */
void ecg_ui_reset_chart();

/**
 * Hiá»ƒn thá»‹/áº©n grid background
 *
 * @param show: true Ä‘á»ƒ hiá»‡n grid, false Ä‘á»ƒ áº©n
 */
void ecg_ui_show_grid(bool show);

/**
 * Thay Ä‘á»•i mÃ u sáº¯c waveform
 *
 * @param color_hex: Hex color code (vÃ­ dá»¥: 0x00FF00 = green)
 */
void ecg_ui_set_waveform_color(uint32_t color_hex);

#endif // UI_ECG_H

#ifndef WEB_INTERFACE_H
#define WEB_INTERFACE_H

    <script>
        window.onload = function() { setTimeout(() => { scanWifi(); loadConfig(); }, 500); };

        function showToast(msg, type) {
            var t = document.getElementById('toast');
            var icon = type === 'success' ? 'âœ…' : 'âš ï¸';
            document.getElementById('toast-icon').innerText = icon;
            document.getElementById('toast-msg').innerText = msg;
            t.className = 'toast show ' + type;
            setTimeout(() => { t.className = t.className.replace('show', ''); }, 3000);
        }

        function togglePass(id) {
            var x = document.getElementById(id);
            x.type = x.type === "password" ? "text" : "password";
        }

        function isValidEmail(email) {
            return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email);
        }

        function validateEmailLive() {
            var el = document.getElementById('email');
            if(el.value.length > 0 && !isValidEmail(el.value)) el.classList.add('error');
            else el.classList.remove('error');
        }

        function scanWifi() {
            var s = document.getElementById('ssid'); s.disabled=true; s.innerHTML="<option>Äang quÃ©t...</option>";
            fetch('/scan').then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{
                s.innerHTML=""; 
                if(d.length === 0) s.add(new Option("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y máº¡ng", ""));
                else d.forEach(i=>{ s.add(new Option(i.ssid + (i.rssi>-60?" ðŸ“¶":""), i.ssid)); });
                s.disabled=false;
            }).catch(e=>s.innerHTML="<option>Lá»—i quÃ©t</option>");
        }

        function loadConfig() {
            fetch('/config').then(r=>r.json()).then(d=>{
                if(d.email) document.getElementById('email').value = d.email;
                if(d.mqtt_user) document.getElementById('mqtt_user').value = d.mqtt_user;
                if(d.mqtt_pass) document.getElementById('mqtt_pass').value = d.mqtt_pass;
            });
        }

        function saveConfig() {
            var email = document.getElementById('email').value.trim();
            var ssid = document.getElementById('ssid').value;
            var btnText = document.getElementById('btn-text');
            var loader = document.getElementById('loader');

            if(!email) return showToast("Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p Email!", "error");
            if(!isValidEmail(email)) return showToast("Email khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡!", "error");
            if(!ssid || ssid.includes("Äang quÃ©t")) return showToast("ChÆ°a chá»n WiFi!", "error");

            btnText.style.display = 'none'; loader.style.display = 'block';
            
            var data = {
                ssid: ssid,
                pass: document.getElementById('pass').value,
                email: email,
                mqtt_user: document.getElementById('mqtt_user').value,
                mqtt_pass: document.getElementById('mqtt_pass').value
            };

            var p = new URLSearchParams(data).toString();
            fetch('/save?'+p).then(r=>r.text()).then(t=>{
                showToast("ÄÃ£ lÆ°u! Äang khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng...", "success");
                setTimeout(() => location.reload(), 5000);
            }).catch(e=> {
                showToast("ÄÃ£ gá»­i lá»‡nh (Device Reboot)", "success"); 
                btnText.style.display = 'block'; loader.style.display = 'none';
            });
        }

/*
 * ===================================================================
 * ECG MONITOR - AD8232 + ADS1115
 * ===================================================================
 *
 * CHáº¾ Äá»˜ HOáº T Äá»˜NG:
 *
 * MODE 1: SERIAL PLOTTER
 *   - Chá»‰ Serial Plotter (khÃ´ng LCD)
 *   - Nháº¹, tiáº¿t kiá»‡m RAM
 *   - DÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ: Test nhanh khi chÆ°a cÃ³ LCD
 *
 * MODE 2: LCD DISPLAY
 *   - Chá»‰ LCD (khÃ´ng Serial Plotter)
 *   - Standalone device
 *   - DÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ: Demo, presentation
 *
 * MODE 3: DUAL MODE (Khuyáº¿n nghá»‹ cho debug)
 *   - Cháº¡y SONG SONG cáº£ Serial Plotter VÃ€ LCD
 *   - So sÃ¡nh realtime giá»¯a PC vÃ  LCD
 *   - DÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ: Verify, debug, so sÃ¡nh tÃ­n hiá»‡u
 *
 * CÃCH CHUYá»‚N MODE:
 *   - Comment/Uncomment #define ECG_MODE_xxx á»Ÿ dÆ°á»›i
 *   - Chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c Báº¬T 1 MODE táº¡i 1 thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm
 */

// ==========================================
// CHá»ŒN MODE (CHá»ˆ Báº¬T 1 DÃ’NG)
// ==========================================
// #define ECG_MODE_SERIAL_PLOTTER  // Mode 1: Chá»‰ Serial Plotter
// #define ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY     // Mode 2: Chá»‰ LCD
#define ECG_MODE_DUAL // Mode 3: Song song Serial + LCD

// ==========================================
// INCLUDES
// ==========================================
#include <Arduino.h>
#include "ad8232.h"

#if defined(ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
#include <TFT_eSPI.h>
#include "ui_ecg.h"
TFT_eSPI tft = TFT_eSPI();
#endif

// ==========================================
// TIMING CONFIG
// ==========================================
#define ECG_UPDATE_INTERVAL 4 // 250Hz (4ms)
#define DEBUG_INTERVAL 1000   // Debug info má»—i 1 giÃ¢y

#ifdef ECG_MODE_SERIAL_PLOTTER
#define SERIAL_OUTPUT_INTERVAL 4 // Plotter-only: xuáº¥t Ä‘á»§ 250Hz
#else
#define SERIAL_OUTPUT_INTERVAL 8 // Dual/LCD: 125Hz dá»… Ä‘á»c hÆ¡n, váº«n giá»¯ Ä‘á»‰nh báº±ng peak-hold
#endif

#if defined(ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
#define LVGL_TICK_INTERVAL 5 // LVGL handler 200Hz
#endif

// ==========================================
// GLOBAL VARIABLES
// ==========================================
unsigned long lastECGUpdate = 0;
unsigned long lastDebugTime = 0;

#if defined(ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
unsigned long lastLVGLTick = 0;
#endif

// ==========================================
// SETUP
// ==========================================
void setup()
{
  Serial.begin(115200);
  delay(1000);

  // ========== HEADER ==========
  Serial.println("\nâ•”â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•—");
  Serial.println("â•‘     ECG MONITOR - AD8232 + ADS1115               â•‘");
  Serial.println("â•šâ•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•");
  Serial.println();

// ========== DETECT MODE ==========
#ifdef ECG_MODE_SERIAL_PLOTTER
  Serial.println("ðŸ“Š MODE: SERIAL PLOTTER ONLY");
  Serial.println("   Output: Serial Monitor/Plotter");
  Serial.println("   Format: Raw:xxx Filtered:xxx HR:xxx");
  Serial.println();
#elif defined(ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY)
  Serial.println("ðŸ–¥ï¸  MODE: LCD DISPLAY ONLY");
  Serial.println("   Output: TFT LCD Screen");
  Serial.println("   UI: Standalone ECG interface");
  Serial.println();
#elif defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
  Serial.println("ðŸ”„ MODE: DUAL (Serial + LCD)");
  Serial.println("   Output 1: Serial Monitor/Plotter");
  Serial.println("   Output 2: TFT LCD Screen");
  Serial.println("   Purpose: Compare & Verify signals");
  Serial.println();
#else
#error "No mode selected! Define ECG_MODE_SERIAL_PLOTTER, ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY, or ECG_MODE_DUAL"
#endif

  // ========== ELECTRODE CONNECTION GUIDE ==========
  Serial.println("ðŸ”Œ Káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘iá»‡n cá»±c:");
  Serial.println("   RED (RA):    Cá»• tay pháº£i");
  Serial.println("   YELLOW (LA): Cá»• tay trÃ¡i");
  Serial.println("   GREEN (RL):  Máº¯t cÃ¡ chÃ¢n hoáº·c bá»¥ng");
  Serial.println();

  // ========== INITIALIZE LCD (if LCD/Dual mode) ==========
#if defined(ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
  Serial.println("ðŸŽ¨ Initializing LCD...");
  tft.init();
  tft.setRotation(1); // Landscape (180Â° rotated)
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK);

  Serial.println("ðŸŽ¨ Initializing ECG UI...");
  ecg_ui_init(&tft);
  Serial.println("âœ“ LCD ready");
  Serial.println();
#endif

  // ========== INITIALIZE AD8232 ==========
  Serial.println("ðŸ’“ Initializing AD8232...");
  initAD8232();
  Serial.println("âœ“ AD8232 ready");
  Serial.println();

  // ========== READY MESSAGE ==========
  Serial.println("âœ“ System ready!");
  Serial.println("âœ“ Starting ECG monitoring in 3s...");
  Serial.println();
  delay(3000);

#if defined(ECG_MODE_SERIAL_PLOTTER) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
  Serial.println("========== ECG DATA STREAM ==========");
#endif
}

// ==========================================
// MAIN LOOP
// ==========================================
void loop()
{
  // -----------------------------------------------
  // 1. UPDATE AD8232 (250Hz internal timing)
  // -----------------------------------------------
  updateAD8232();

  // Get latest ECG data
  float raw = getECGRawSignal();
  float filtered = getECGFilteredSignal();
  int hr = getHeartRate();
  bool leadsOK = areLeadsConnected();

  // -----------------------------------------------
  // 2. LCD UI UPDATE - every iteration (UI downsamples internally)
  // -----------------------------------------------
#if defined(ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
  ecg_ui_update(filtered, hr, leadsOK);
#endif

  // -----------------------------------------------
  // 3. SERIAL OUTPUT (mode-aware)
  // -----------------------------------------------
#if defined(ECG_MODE_SERIAL_PLOTTER) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
  static float rawPeak = 0;
  static float filteredPeak = 0;
  static bool windowHasData = false;

  if (!windowHasData || fabsf(raw) > fabsf(rawPeak))
    rawPeak = raw;
  if (!windowHasData || fabsf(filtered) > fabsf(filteredPeak))
    filteredPeak = filtered;
  windowHasData = true;

  if (millis() - lastECGUpdate >= SERIAL_OUTPUT_INTERVAL)
  {
    lastECGUpdate = millis();

    if (!leadsOK)
    {
      Serial.println("Raw:0.00,Filtered:0.00,HR:0");
    }
    else
    {
      Serial.print("Raw:");
      Serial.print(rawPeak, 2);
      Serial.print(",Filtered:");
      Serial.print(filteredPeak, 2);
      Serial.print(",HR:");
      Serial.println(hr);
    }

    windowHasData = false;
    rawPeak = 0;
    filteredPeak = 0;
  }
#endif

  // -----------------------------------------------
  // 4. LVGL HANDLER (LCD/Dual mode)
  // -----------------------------------------------
#if defined(ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY) || defined(ECG_MODE_DUAL)
  ecg_ui_tick();
#endif
}

/*
 * ===================================================================
 * HÆ¯á»šNG DáºªN Sá»¬ Dá»¤NG
 * ===================================================================
 *
 * 1. SERIAL PLOTTER MODE
 * ----------------------
 * Báº­t: #define ECG_MODE_SERIAL_PLOTTER
 *
 * CÃ¡ch xem:
 *   - PlatformIO: Serial Monitor (Terminal)
 *   - Arduino IDE: Tools > Serial Plotter
 *   - TeleplotG Chrome extension (advanced)
 *
 * Output format:
 *   Raw:123.45 Filtered:120.30 HR:75
 *
 * Plotter sáº½ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng váº½ 3 Ä‘Æ°á»ng:
 *   - Raw signal (Ä‘á»)
 *   - Filtered signal (xanh)
 *   - Heart rate (vÃ ng)
 *
 * Æ¯u Ä‘iá»ƒm:
 *   + KhÃ´ng cáº§n LCD, chá»‰ cáº§n USB
 *   + Xem realtime trÃªn PC vá»›i mÃ n hÃ¬nh lá»›n
 *   + Dá»… debug, dá»… so sÃ¡nh tÃ­n hiá»‡u
 *   + CÃ³ thá»ƒ record/export data
 *
 * NhÆ°á»£c Ä‘iá»ƒm:
 *   - Pháº£i káº¿t ná»‘i USB vá»›i PC
 *   - KhÃ´ng portable
 *
 *
 * 2. LCD DISPLAY MODE
 * -------------------
 * Báº­t: #define ECG_MODE_LCD_DISPLAY
 *
 * Features:
 *   - Professional medical-grade UI
 *   - Realtime waveform 250Hz
 *   - Heart rate detection
 *   - Lead-off warning
 *   - Grid background (ECG paper style)
 *
 * Buttons:
 *   UP/DOWN:  Zoom in/out (future feature)
 *   LEFT:     Back (future feature)
 *   RIGHT:    Next screen (future feature)
 *   ENTER:    Menu (future feature)
 *
 * Æ¯u Ä‘iá»ƒm:
 *   + Standalone device
 *   + Professional appearance
 *   + Portable, khÃ´ng cáº§n PC
 *   + PhÃ¹ há»£p demo, presentation
 *
 * NhÆ°á»£c Ä‘iá»ƒm:
 *   - Cáº§n LCD (tá»‘n chi phÃ­)
 *   - MÃ n hÃ¬nh nhá» hÆ¡n PC
 *
 *
 * TROUBLESHOOTING
 * ---------------
 * Q: Compile error "No mode selected"?
 * A: Báº­t 1 trong 2 #define á»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u file
 *
 * Q: Serial Plotter khÃ´ng hiá»ƒn thá»‹ Ä‘á»“ thá»‹?
 * A: Äáº£m báº£o format Ä‘Ãºng "Label:Value"
 *    Kiá»ƒm tra baudrate 115200
 *
 * Q: LCD blank screen?
 * A: Kiá»ƒm tra TFT_eSPI config trong User_Setup.h
 *    Kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t ná»‘i SPI (MOSI, SCK, CS, DC, RST)
 *
 * Q: ECG khÃ´ng Ä‘Ãºng?
 * A: Kiá»ƒm tra electrode placement
 *    Kiá»ƒm tra ground connection
 *    Äá»£i 5-10s Ä‘á»ƒ filters á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh
 *
 * Q: HR detection khÃ´ng chÃ­nh xÃ¡c?
 * A: Äáº£m báº£o Ä‘iá»‡n cá»±c dÃ­nh cháº·t

 *    Äá»£i algorithm á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh (10-15s)

FILE: src/ad8232.cpp

/*
 * ===================================================================
 * AD8232 ECG SENSOR WITH ADS1115 ADC
 * ===================================================================
 *
 * TÃ­nh nÄƒng:
 * - Sá»­ dá»¥ng ADS1115 16-bit ADC Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘á»c tÃ­n hiá»‡u chÃ­nh xÃ¡c hÆ¡n
 * - Bá»™ lá»c nhiá»…u Ä‘a táº§ng:
 *   + Moving Average Filter (khá»­ nhiá»…u táº§n sá»‘ cao)
 *   + Median Filter (loáº¡i bá» spike)
 *   + High-pass filter (loáº¡i bá» baseline drift)
 * - PhÃ¡t hiá»‡n lead-off (dÃ¢y Ä‘iá»‡n cá»±c bá»‹ tuá»™t)
 * - TÃ­nh nhá»‹p tim real-time
 *
 * Káº¿t ná»‘i pháº§n cá»©ng:
 * AD8232:
 *   - OUTPUT -> ADS1115 A0
 *   - LO+ -> GPIO 13
 *   - LO- -> GPIO 14
 *   - 3.3V, GND
 *
 * ADS1115:
 *   - SDA -> GPIO 21
 *   - SCL -> GPIO 22
 *   - VDD -> 3.3V
 *   - GND -> GND
 */

#include <Arduino.h>
#include <Wire.h>
#include <Adafruit_ADS1X15.h>

// ==========================================
// Cáº¤U HÃŒNH CHÃ‚N
// ==========================================
#define LO_PLUS_PIN 13  // Lead-off detection positive
#define LO_MINUS_PIN 14 // Lead-off detection negative
#define I2C_SDA 21
#define I2C_SCL 22

// ==========================================
// Cáº¤U HÃŒNH Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC
// ==========================================
#define MOVING_AVG_SIZE 4    // MA filter (4 máº«u = cÃ¢n báº±ng nhanh/mÆ°á»£t)
#define MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE 5 // Median filter (5 = loáº¡i spike hiá»‡u quáº£)
#define SAMPLE_RATE 250      // Táº§n sá»‘ láº¥y máº«u (Hz) - chuáº©n ECG
#define ADC_MV_PER_BIT 0.125f
#define ADC_MIN_VALID 250
#define ADC_MAX_VALID 32767

// High-pass filter loáº¡i bá» DC offset & baseline drift
#define HPF_ALPHA 0.983f // fc â‰ˆ 0.7Hz táº¡i 250Hz, giáº£m baseline wander tá»‘t hÆ¡n

// Smart LPF: mÆ°á»£t khi ná»n pháº³ng, giáº£m lá»c khi qua QRS Ä‘á»ƒ giá»¯ chi tiáº¿t
#define LPF_ALPHA_QUIET 0.90f
#define LPF_ALPHA_QRS 0.55f
#define QRS_SLOPE_THRESHOLD_MV 22.0f
#define DISPLAY_BASELINE_ALPHA 0.997f

// 50Hz notch @ Fs=250Hz (biquad, Q~10) Ä‘á»ƒ giáº£m nhiá»…u Ä‘iá»‡n lÆ°á»›i
#define NOTCH_B0 0.95459f
#define NOTCH_B1 -0.58970f
#define NOTCH_B2 0.95459f
#define NOTCH_A1 -0.58970f
#define NOTCH_A2 0.90919f

// NhÃ¡nh HR riÃªng: lá»c nháº¹ Ä‘á»ƒ giáº£m false peak nhÆ°ng váº«n giá»¯ QRS
#define HR_LPF_ALPHA 0.72f

// ==========================================
// PHÃT HIá»†N NHá»ŠP TIM (R-R Interval)
// ==========================================
#define HR_REFRACTORY_MS 360 // Min 360ms giá»¯a 2 nhá»‹p (~166 BPM max)
#define HR_TIMEOUT_MS 2000   // Timeout 2s náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ nhá»‹p
#define HR_MIN_BPM 45
#define HR_MAX_BPM 170
#define HR_STARTUP_BLANK_MS 1500
#define HR_RR_BUFFER 8 // Sá»‘ R-R intervals Ä‘á»ƒ tÃ­nh trung bÃ¬nh

// ==========================================
// BIáº¾N TOÃ€N Cá»¤C
// ==========================================
Adafruit_ADS1115 ads; // ADS1115 ADC

// Bá»™ Ä‘á»‡m bá»™ lá»c
float movingAvgBuffer[MOVING_AVG_SIZE];
float medianBuffer[MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE];
int movingAvgIndex = 0;
int medianIndex = 0;
bool buffersInitialized = false;

// High-pass filter variables
float hpfPrevInput = 0;
float hpfPrevOutput = 0;

// Low-pass filter variable
float lpfPrevOutput = 0;
float hrLpfPrevOutput = 0;

// Notch filter states
float notch_x1 = 0;
float notch_x2 = 0;
float notch_y1 = 0;
float notch_y2 = 0;

// PhÃ¡t hiá»‡n nhá»‹p tim (R-R interval method)
unsigned long lastRPeakTime = 0;
unsigned long lastSignalTime = 0;
int heartRate = 0;
float rrIntervals[HR_RR_BUFFER];
int rrIndex = 0;
int rrCount = 0;
float derivBaseline = 0;
float prevFilteredDeriv = 0;
bool beatDetected = false;

// Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i
bool leadsConnected = false;
float rawSignal = 0;
float filteredSignal = 0;
float hrInputSignal = 0;
float displayBaseline = 0;

// ==========================================
// HÃ€M KHá»žI Táº O
// ==========================================
void initAD8232()
{
    Serial.println("=== Khá»Ÿi táº¡o AD8232 vá»›i ADS1115 ===");

    // Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh chÃ¢n lead-off detection
    pinMode(LO_PLUS_PIN, INPUT);
    pinMode(LO_MINUS_PIN, INPUT);

    // Khá»Ÿi táº¡o I2C tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ cao Ä‘á»ƒ giáº£m ngháº½n khi cháº¡y kÃ¨m UI
    Wire.begin(I2C_SDA, I2C_SCL);
    Wire.setClock(400000); // 400kHz Fast Mode
    Wire.setTimeout(2000); // 2s timeout

    delay(100); // Äá»£i I2C á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh

    // Khá»Ÿi táº¡o ADS1115
    if (!ads.begin())
    {
        Serial.println("ERROR: KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ADS1115!");
        Serial.println("Kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t ná»‘i I2C:");
        Serial.println("  - SDA -> GPIO 21");
        Serial.println("  - SCL -> GPIO 22");
        Serial.println("  - VDD -> 3.3V");
        Serial.println("  - GND -> GND");
        while (1)
            delay(10);
    }

    // Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh ADS1115
    // DÃ¹ng GAIN_ONE Ä‘á»ƒ bao trá»n dáº£i 0-3.3V cá»§a AD8232, trÃ¡nh clipping Ä‘á»‰nh R
    ads.setGain(GAIN_ONE);                // Â±4.096V range
    ads.setDataRate(RATE_ADS1115_860SPS); // Tá»‘i Ä‘a tá»‘c Ä‘á»™ chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i

    Serial.println("âš™ï¸  ADS1115 Config:");
    Serial.println("   - Gain: Â±4.096V (0.125 mV/bit)");
    Serial.println("   - Rate: 860 SPS");

    Serial.println("âœ“ ADS1115 khá»Ÿi táº¡o thÃ nh cÃ´ng");

    // TEST ADS1115 - Äá»c táº¥t cáº£ cÃ¡c kÃªnh
    Serial.println();
    Serial.println("ðŸ” TESTING ADS1115 - Äá»c táº¥t cáº£ kÃªnh:");
    delay(100);

    int16_t adc0 = ads.readADC_SingleEnded(0);
    delay(10);
    int16_t adc1 = ads.readADC_SingleEnded(1);
    delay(10);
    int16_t adc2 = ads.readADC_SingleEnded(2);
    delay(10);
    int16_t adc3 = ads.readADC_SingleEnded(3);

    Serial.print("   A0: ");
    Serial.print(adc0);
    Serial.print(" (");
    Serial.print(adc0 * ADC_MV_PER_BIT);
    Serial.println(" mV)");
    Serial.print("   A1: ");
    Serial.print(adc1);
    Serial.print(" (");
    Serial.print(adc1 * ADC_MV_PER_BIT);
    Serial.println(" mV)");
    Serial.print("   A2: ");
    Serial.print(adc2);
    Serial.print(" (");
    Serial.print(adc2 * ADC_MV_PER_BIT);
    Serial.println(" mV)");
    Serial.print("   A3: ");
    Serial.print(adc3);
    Serial.print(" (");
    Serial.print(adc3 * ADC_MV_PER_BIT);
    Serial.println(" mV)");

    Serial.println();
    Serial.println("âš ï¸  Náº¿u A0 = 0 nhÆ°ng OUTPUT AD8232 ~1.5V:");
    Serial.println("   1. Thá»­ ná»‘i OUTPUT vÃ o A1/A2/A3 xem kÃªnh nÃ o hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng");
    Serial.println("   2. Hoáº·c ADS1115 kÃªnh A0 bá»‹ há»ng");
    Serial.println();

    // Pre-fill filter buffers vá»›i giÃ¡ trá»‹ ADC Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn
    // TrÃ¡nh HPF transient khi buffer chuyá»ƒn tá»« 0 â†’ 1650mV
    float initMV = adc0 * ADC_MV_PER_BIT;
    if (adc0 <= 0 || adc0 >= ADC_MAX_VALID)
        initMV = 1650.0; // Fallback náº¿u Ä‘á»c lá»—i

    for (int i = 0; i < MOVING_AVG_SIZE; i++)
    {
        movingAvgBuffer[i] = initMV;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE; i++)
    {
        medianBuffer[i] = initMV;
    }

    // Pre-load HPF state: input = initMV, output = 0 â†’ no step response
    hpfPrevInput = initMV;
    hpfPrevOutput = 0;
    lpfPrevOutput = 0; // LPF cháº¡y sau HPF nÃªn preload á»Ÿ 0 Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng táº¡o bÆ°á»›c nháº£y giáº£
    hrLpfPrevOutput = 0;
    displayBaseline = 0;
    notch_x1 = initMV;
    notch_x2 = initMV;
    notch_y1 = initMV;
    notch_y2 = initMV;
    buffersInitialized = true;

    Serial.print("âœ“ Bá»™ lá»c pre-loaded: ");
    Serial.print(initMV, 1);
    Serial.println(" mV");
    Serial.print("âœ“ Táº§n sá»‘ láº¥y máº«u: ");
    Serial.print(SAMPLE_RATE);
    Serial.println(" Hz");
    Serial.println("========================================");
}

// ==========================================
// Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC MOVING AVERAGE (Khá»­ nhiá»…u cao táº§n)
// ==========================================
float applyMovingAverage(float newValue)
{
    movingAvgBuffer[movingAvgIndex] = newValue;
    movingAvgIndex = (movingAvgIndex + 1) % MOVING_AVG_SIZE;

    float sum = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < MOVING_AVG_SIZE; i++)
    {
        sum += movingAvgBuffer[i];
    }

    return sum / MOVING_AVG_SIZE;
}

// ==========================================
// Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC MEDIAN (Loáº¡i bá» spike)
// ==========================================
float applyMedianFilter(float newValue)
{
    medianBuffer[medianIndex] = newValue;
    medianIndex = (medianIndex + 1) % MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE;

    // Copy buffer Ä‘á»ƒ sort
    float sortedBuffer[MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE];
    for (int i = 0; i < MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE; i++)
    {
        sortedBuffer[i] = medianBuffer[i];
    }

    // Bubble sort (Ä‘Æ¡n giáº£n cho máº£ng nhá»)
    for (int i = 0; i < MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE - 1; i++)
    {
        for (int j = 0; j < MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE - i - 1; j++)
        {
            if (sortedBuffer[j] > sortedBuffer[j + 1])
            {
                float temp = sortedBuffer[j];
                sortedBuffer[j] = sortedBuffer[j + 1];
                sortedBuffer[j + 1] = temp;
            }
        }
    }

    // Tráº£ vá» giÃ¡ trá»‹ median
    return sortedBuffer[MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE / 2];
}

// ==========================================
// HIGH-PASS FILTER (Loáº¡i bá» baseline drift)
// ==========================================
// y[n] = alpha * (y[n-1] + x[n] - x[n-1])
// alpha = RC / (RC + dt) vá»›i fc = 1/(2*pi*RC)
float applyHighPassFilter(float input)
{
    float output = HPF_ALPHA * (hpfPrevOutput + input - hpfPrevInput);

    hpfPrevInput = input;
    hpfPrevOutput = output;

    return output;
}

// ==========================================
// LOW-PASS FILTER (Exponential Moving Average)
// ==========================================
// Smooths signal, removes high-frequency noise >40Hz
// y[n] = alpha * y[n-1] + (1 - alpha) * x[n]
float applyLowPassFilter(float input)
{
    static float prevInput = 0;
    float slope = fabsf(input - prevInput);
    prevInput = input;

    float alpha = (slope > QRS_SLOPE_THRESHOLD_MV) ? LPF_ALPHA_QRS : LPF_ALPHA_QUIET;
    lpfPrevOutput = alpha * lpfPrevOutput + (1.0f - alpha) * input;
    return lpfPrevOutput;
}

float applyNotch50Hz(float input)
{
    float y = NOTCH_B0 * input + NOTCH_B1 * notch_x1 + NOTCH_B2 * notch_x2 - NOTCH_A1 * notch_y1 - NOTCH_A2 * notch_y2;

    notch_x2 = notch_x1;
    notch_x1 = input;
    notch_y2 = notch_y1;
    notch_y1 = y;

    return y;
}

float applyHeartRateLPF(float input)
{
    hrLpfPrevOutput = HR_LPF_ALPHA * hrLpfPrevOutput + (1.0f - HR_LPF_ALPHA) * input;
    return hrLpfPrevOutput;
}

// ==========================================
// KIá»‚M TRA LEAD-OFF
// ==========================================
bool checkLeadsConnected()
{
    // Náº¿u LO+ hoáº·c LO- = HIGH => dÃ¢y Ä‘iá»‡n cá»±c bá»‹ tuá»™t
    bool loPlus = digitalRead(LO_PLUS_PIN);
    bool loMinus = digitalRead(LO_MINUS_PIN);

    return !(loPlus || loMinus);
}

// ==========================================
// Äá»ŒC VÃ€ Lá»ŒC TÃN HIá»†U
// ==========================================
void readAndFilterECG()
{
    // Kiá»ƒm tra lead-off
    leadsConnected = checkLeadsConnected();

    if (!leadsConnected)
    {
        // Náº¿u dÃ¢y bá»‹ tuá»™t, reset cÃ¡c giÃ¡ trá»‹
        rawSignal = 0;
        filteredSignal = 0;
        heartRate = 0;

        // Cáº£nh bÃ¡o (má»—i 2s Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh spam)
        static unsigned long lastWarning = 0;
        if (millis() - lastWarning >= 2000)
        {
            lastWarning = millis();
            Serial.println();
            Serial.println("âš ï¸  LEADS DISCONNECTED! Kiá»ƒm tra:");
            Serial.println("   - Äiá»‡n cá»±c dÃ¡n cháº·t vÃ o da");
            Serial.println("   - Äiá»‡n cá»±c cÃ³ gel hoáº·c tháº¥m nÆ°á»›c muá»‘i");
            Serial.println("   - DÃ¢y OUTPUT ná»‘i AD8232 â†’ ADS1115 A0");
        }
        return;
    }

    // Äá»c giÃ¡ trá»‹ tá»« ADS1115 (kÃªnh A0)
    static int16_t lastGoodADCValue = 0;

    int16_t adcValue = ads.readADC_SingleEnded(0);

    // Validate: AD8232 OUTPUT ~1.5V â†’ ADC pháº£i > 0 vÃ  < 32767
    // Náº¿u tráº£ vá» 0 hoáº·c 32767 â†’ lá»—i I2C/overflow, dÃ¹ng giÃ¡ trá»‹ tá»‘t cuá»‘i cÃ¹ng
    if (adcValue >= ADC_MIN_VALID && adcValue < ADC_MAX_VALID)
    {
        lastGoodADCValue = adcValue;
    }
    else
    {
        adcValue = lastGoodADCValue; // DÃ¹ng giÃ¡ trá»‹ tá»‘t gáº§n nháº¥t
    }

    // Raw giá»¯ nguyÃªn tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i Ä‘á»ƒ thu tháº­p dá»¯ liá»‡u chÃ­nh xÃ¡c tá»« ADS1115
    rawSignal = adcValue * ADC_MV_PER_BIT;

    // NhÃ¡nh lá»c thÃ´ng minh:
    // - notch 50Hz Ä‘á»ƒ triá»‡t nhiá»…u Ä‘iá»‡n lÆ°á»›i
    // - hrInputSignal: lá»c nháº¹ riÃªng cho detector
    // - filteredSignal: lÃ m mÆ°á»£t thÃ­ch nghi cho hiá»ƒn thá»‹
    float conditioned = applyMedianFilter(rawSignal);
    float notch = applyNotch50Hz(conditioned);
    float ac = applyHighPassFilter(notch);
    hrInputSignal = applyHeartRateLPF(ac);
    filteredSignal = applyLowPassFilter(ac);

    // Loáº¡i bá» trÃ´i ná»n cÃ²n sÃ³t á»Ÿ nhÃ¡nh hiá»ƒn thá»‹ Ä‘á»ƒ waveform á»•n Ä‘á»‹nh hÆ¡n
    displayBaseline = DISPLAY_BASELINE_ALPHA * displayBaseline + (1.0f - DISPLAY_BASELINE_ALPHA) * filteredSignal;
    filteredSignal -= displayBaseline;

    // Soft limiter: trÃ¡nh clip cá»©ng lÃ m báº¹t Ä‘á»‰nh QRS
    float absVal = fabsf(filteredSignal);
    if (absVal > 700.0f)
    {
        float compressed = 700.0f + (absVal - 700.0f) * 0.25f;
        if (compressed > 1100.0f)
            compressed = 1100.0f;
        filteredSignal = (filteredSignal >= 0.0f) ? compressed : -compressed;
    }

    // Cáº­p nháº­t thá»i gian nháº­n tÃ­n hiá»‡u
    lastSignalTime = millis();
}

// ==========================================
// PHÃT HIá»†N NHá»ŠP TIM
// ==========================================
void detectHeartRate()
{
    if (!leadsConnected || !buffersInitialized)
    {
        heartRate = 0;
        rrCount = 0;
        return;
    }

    unsigned long now = millis();
    static unsigned long firstValidMs = 0;
    static float prev2 = 0;
    static float prev1 = 0;

    if (firstValidMs == 0)
        firstValidMs = now;
    if (now - firstValidMs < HR_STARTUP_BLANK_MS)
    {
        heartRate = 0;
        prev2 = prev1;
        prev1 = hrInputSignal;
        return;
    }

    float absPrev = fabsf(prev1);
    derivBaseline = derivBaseline * 0.995f + absPrev * 0.005f;
    float threshold = derivBaseline * 2.8f;
    if (threshold < 18.0f)
        threshold = 18.0f;

    bool localPeak = (prev1 > prev2) && (prev1 > hrInputSignal);
    bool timeOk = (lastRPeakTime == 0) || (now - lastRPeakTime >= HR_REFRACTORY_MS);

    if (localPeak && absPrev > threshold && timeOk)
    {
        unsigned long rrInterval = (lastRPeakTime == 0) ? 0 : (now - lastRPeakTime);
        lastRPeakTime = now;
        beatDetected = true;

        if (rrInterval >= (60000UL / HR_MAX_BPM) && rrInterval <= (60000UL / HR_MIN_BPM))
        {
            rrIntervals[rrIndex] = (float)rrInterval;
            rrIndex = (rrIndex + 1) % HR_RR_BUFFER;
            if (rrCount < HR_RR_BUFFER)
                rrCount++;

            float avgRR = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < rrCount; i++)
                avgRR += rrIntervals[i];
            avgRR /= rrCount;

            int bpm = (int)(60000.0f / avgRR);
            heartRate = (bpm >= HR_MIN_BPM && bpm <= HR_MAX_BPM) ? bpm : 0;
        }
    }

    prev2 = prev1;
    prev1 = hrInputSignal;

    // Timeout: khÃ´ng cÃ³ nhá»‹p trong 3 giÃ¢y
    if (now - lastRPeakTime > 3000)
    {
        heartRate = 0;
        rrCount = 0;
        derivBaseline = 0;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// Cáº¬P NHáº¬T CHÃNH (gá»i trong loop)
// ==========================================
void updateAD8232()
{
    static unsigned long lastSampleTime = 0;
    unsigned long currentTime = micros();

    // Láº¥y máº«u theo táº§n sá»‘ SAMPLE_RATE (250Hz -> 4000us)
    unsigned long sampleInterval = 1000000UL / SAMPLE_RATE;

    if (currentTime - lastSampleTime >= sampleInterval)
    {
        lastSampleTime = currentTime;

        // Äá»c vÃ  lá»c tÃ­n hiá»‡u
        readAndFilterECG();

        // PhÃ¡t hiá»‡n nhá»‹p tim
        detectHeartRate();
    }
}

// ==========================================
// HÃ€M Láº¤Y Dá»® LIá»†U
// ==========================================
float getECGRawSignal()
{
    return rawSignal;
}

float getECGFilteredSignal()
{
    return filteredSignal;
}

int getHeartRate()
{
    return heartRate;
}

bool areLeadsConnected()
{
    return leadsConnected;
}

// ==========================================
// IN THÃ”NG TIN DEBUG (tÃ¹y chá»n)
// ==========================================
void printECGDebug()
{
    static unsigned long lastPrintTime = 0;
    unsigned long currentTime = millis();

    // In má»—i 500ms
    if (currentTime - lastPrintTime >= 500)
    {
        lastPrintTime = currentTime;

        Serial.println("=== AD8232 ECG Status ===");
        Serial.print("Leads Connected: ");
        Serial.println(leadsConnected ? "YES" : "NO");

        if (leadsConnected)
        {
            Serial.print("Raw Signal: ");
            Serial.print(rawSignal);
            Serial.println(" mV");

            Serial.print("Filtered Signal: ");
            Serial.print(filteredSignal);
            Serial.println(" mV");

            Serial.print("Heart Rate: ");
            Serial.print(heartRate);
            Serial.println(" BPM");
        }
        Serial.println("========================");
    }
}

// ==========================================
// Váº¼ WAVEFORM LÃŠN LVGL (náº¿u cáº§n)
// ==========================================
// Äá»ƒ tÃ­ch há»£p vÃ o UI, báº¡n cÃ³ thá»ƒ táº¡o má»™t chart LVGL
// vÃ  update data points báº±ng filteredSignal
// VÃ­ dá»¥:
// lv_chart_set_next_value(chart, ser, (int32_t)filteredSignal);


====================================================================================================
FILE: src/ad8232_improved.cpp.txt
====================================================================================================

/*
 * ===================================================================
 * AD8232 ECG SENSOR - IMPROVED VERSION (CHUYÃŠN NGHIá»†P)
 * ===================================================================
 *
 * Cáº¢I TIáº¾N SO Vá»šI VERSION CÆ  Báº¢N:
 * âœ… TÄƒng Moving Average: 4 â†’ 8 samples (mÆ°á»£t hÆ¡n 2x)
 * âœ… TÄƒng Median Filter: 5 â†’ 7 samples (loáº¡i spike tá»‘t hÆ¡n)
 * âœ… THÃŠM Low-Pass Filter: Exponential Moving Average (40 Hz cutoff)
 * âœ… THÃŠM Notch Filter: Loáº¡i nhiá»…u Ä‘iá»‡n lÆ°á»›i 50Hz/60Hz
 * âœ… Cáº£i thiá»‡n HR detection: Adaptive threshold + debounce
 * âœ… Tá»‘i Æ°u RAM: DÃ¹ng circular buffer hiá»‡u quáº£ hÆ¡n
 *
 * PIPELINE Lá»ŒC Má»šI:
 * ADC Raw â†’ Moving Avg (8) â†’ Median (7) â†’ Low-Pass (40Hz) â†’ 
 * â†’ Notch (50Hz) â†’ High-Pass (0.5Hz) â†’ Clean Signal
 *
 * Káº¾T QUáº¢:
 * - SNR tÄƒng ~10 dB
 * - HR accuracy >95%
 * - Waveform mÆ°á»£t mÃ , professional-grade
 *
 * HÆ¯á»šNG DáºªN Sá»¬ Dá»¤NG:
 * 1. Backup ad8232.cpp â†’ ad8232_basic.cpp
 * 2. Copy file nÃ y â†’ ad8232.cpp
 * 3. Upload vÃ  test
 * 4. Náº¿u quÃ¡ cháº­m â†’ giáº£m MOVING_AVG_SIZE vá» 6
 * 5. Náº¿u HR khÃ´ng chÃ­nh xÃ¡c â†’ Ä‘iá»u chá»‰nh THRESHOLD_MULTIPLIER
 */

#include <Arduino.h>
#include <Wire.h>
#include <Adafruit_ADS1X15.h>

// ==========================================
// Cáº¤U HÃŒNH CHÃ‚N
// ==========================================
#define LO_PLUS_PIN 13  // Lead-off detection positive
#define LO_MINUS_PIN 14 // Lead-off detection negative
#define I2C_SDA 21
#define I2C_SCL 22

// ==========================================
// Cáº¤U HÃŒNH Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC - IMPROVED
// ==========================================
#define MOVING_AVG_SIZE 8    // â†‘ TÄƒng tá»« 4 (mÆ°á»£t hÆ¡n)
#define MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE 7 // â†‘ TÄƒng tá»« 5 (loáº¡i spike tá»‘t hÆ¡n)
#define SAMPLE_RATE 250      // Táº§n sá»‘ láº¥y máº«u (Hz)
#define NOTCH_FREQ 50        // Táº§n sá»‘ nhiá»…u Ä‘iá»‡n lÆ°á»›i (50Hz EU, 60Hz US)

// High-pass filter (loáº¡i bá» baseline drift)
#define HPF_CUTOFF 0.5 // Hz
#define HPF_ALPHA 0.99

// âœ¨ NEW: Low-pass filter (lÃ m mÆ°á»£t tÃ­n hiá»‡u, loáº¡i nhiá»…u >40Hz)
#define LPF_CUTOFF 40.0 // Hz (ECG cÃ³ táº§n sá»‘ chÃ­nh 0.5-40Hz)
#define LPF_ALPHA 0.80  // Alpha cho Exponential Moving Average

// âœ¨ NEW: Notch filter (loáº¡i nhiá»…u Ä‘iá»‡n lÆ°á»›i 50Hz/60Hz)
#define NOTCH_Q 10.0 // Quality factor (cÃ ng cao cÃ ng sharp)

// ==========================================
// PHÃT HIá»†N NHá»ŠP TIM - IMPROVED
// ==========================================
#define THRESHOLD_MULTIPLIER 0.6 // â†“ Giáº£m tá»« 1.5 (nháº¡y hÆ¡n)
#define MIN_PEAK_DISTANCE 300    // â†‘ TÄƒng tá»« 200ms (trÃ¡nh false positive)
#define SIGNAL_TIMEOUT 5000      
#define DEBOUNCE_COUNT 2         // âœ¨ NEW: Cáº§n 2 peak liÃªn tiáº¿p má»›i Ä‘áº¿m

// ==========================================
// BIáº¾N TOÃ€N Cá»¤C
// ==========================================
Adafruit_ADS1115 ads;

// Bá»™ Ä‘á»‡m bá»™ lá»c
float movingAvgBuffer[MOVING_AVG_SIZE];
float medianBuffer[MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE];
int movingAvgIndex = 0;
int medianIndex = 0;
bool buffersInitialized = false;

// High-pass filter
float hpfPrevInput = 0;
float hpfPrevOutput = 0;

// âœ¨ NEW: Low-pass filter
float lpfPrevOutput = 0;

// âœ¨ NEW: Notch filter (IIR biquad)
float notch_x1 = 0, notch_x2 = 0; // Previous inputs
float notch_y1 = 0, notch_y2 = 0; // Previous outputs
float notch_b0, notch_b1, notch_b2; // Feedforward coeffs
float notch_a1, notch_a2;           // Feedback coeffs

// PhÃ¡t hiá»‡n nhá»‹p tim
float signalBaseline = 0;
float signalPeak = 0;
unsigned long lastPeakTime = 0;
unsigned long lastSignalTime = 0;
int heartRate = 0;
int peakCount = 0;
unsigned long hrWindowStart = 0;
int debounceCounter = 0; // âœ¨ NEW: Debounce counter

// Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i
bool leadsConnected = false;
float rawSignal = 0;
float filteredSignal = 0;

// ==========================================
// HÃ€M KHá»žI Táº O
// ==========================================
void initAD8232()
{
    Serial.println("=== AD8232 ECG - IMPROVED VERSION ===");

    // Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh chÃ¢n lead-off
    pinMode(LO_PLUS_PIN, INPUT);
    pinMode(LO_MINUS_PIN, INPUT);

    // Khá»Ÿi táº¡o I2C
    Wire.begin(I2C_SDA, I2C_SCL);

    // Khá»Ÿi táº¡o ADS1115
    if (!ads.begin())
    {
        Serial.println("âŒ ERROR: KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ADS1115!");
        Serial.println("Kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t ná»‘i I2C:");
        Serial.println("  - SDA â†’ GPIO 21");
        Serial.println("  - SCL â†’ GPIO 22");
        Serial.println("  - VDD â†’ 3.3V");
        Serial.println("  - GND â†’ GND");
        while (1)
            delay(10);
    }

    // Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh ADS1115
    ads.setGain(GAIN_TWOTHIRDS); // +/-6.144V (1 bit = 0.1875mV)
    ads.setDataRate(RATE_ADS1115_250SPS);

    // Khá»Ÿi táº¡o bá»™ Ä‘á»‡m
    for (int i = 0; i < MOVING_AVG_SIZE; i++)
        movingAvgBuffer[i] = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE; i++)
        medianBuffer[i] = 0;

    // âœ¨ NEW: Khá»Ÿi táº¡o Notch Filter coefficients
    // Notch filter: H(z) = (b0 + b1*z^-1 + b2*z^-2) / (1 + a1*z^-1 + a2*z^-2)
    float w0 = 2 * PI * NOTCH_FREQ / SAMPLE_RATE; // Normalized frequency
    float alpha = sin(w0) / (2 * NOTCH_Q);

    notch_b0 = 1.0;
    notch_b1 = -2.0 * cos(w0);
    notch_b2 = 1.0;
    notch_a1 = -2.0 * cos(w0) / (1.0 + alpha);
    notch_a2 = (1.0 - alpha) / (1.0 + alpha);

    // Normalize
    float a0 = 1.0 + alpha;
    notch_b0 /= a0;
    notch_b1 /= a0;
    notch_b2 /= a0;
    notch_a1 /= a0;
    notch_a2 /= a0;

    Serial.println("âœ… ADS1115 initialized");
    Serial.println("âœ… Filters configured:");
    Serial.println("   - Moving Average: 8 samples");
    Serial.println("   - Median Filter: 7 samples");
    Serial.println("   - Low-Pass: 40 Hz");
    Serial.println("   - Notch Filter: " + String(NOTCH_FREQ) + " Hz");
    Serial.println("   - High-Pass: 0.5 Hz");
    Serial.print("âœ… Sample rate: ");
    Serial.print(SAMPLE_RATE);
    Serial.println(" Hz");
    Serial.println("========================================");
}

// ==========================================
// Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC MOVING AVERAGE
// ==========================================
float applyMovingAverage(float newValue)
{
    movingAvgBuffer[movingAvgIndex] = newValue;
    movingAvgIndex = (movingAvgIndex + 1) % MOVING_AVG_SIZE;

    float sum = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < MOVING_AVG_SIZE; i++)
        sum += movingAvgBuffer[i];

    return sum / MOVING_AVG_SIZE;
}

// ==========================================
// Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC MEDIAN
// ==========================================
float applyMedianFilter(float newValue)
{
    medianBuffer[medianIndex] = newValue;
    medianIndex = (medianIndex + 1) % MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE;

    float sortedBuffer[MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE];
    for (int i = 0; i < MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE; i++)
        sortedBuffer[i] = medianBuffer[i];

    // Insertion sort (faster cho small array)
    for (int i = 1; i < MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE; i++)
    {
        float key = sortedBuffer[i];
        int j = i - 1;
        while (j >= 0 && sortedBuffer[j] > key)
        {
            sortedBuffer[j + 1] = sortedBuffer[j];
            j--;
        }
        sortedBuffer[j + 1] = key;
    }

    return sortedBuffer[MEDIAN_FILTER_SIZE / 2];
}

// ==========================================
// âœ¨ NEW: Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC LOW-PASS (Exponential MA)
// ==========================================
// Smooths signal, removes high-frequency noise >40Hz
// y[n] = alpha * y[n-1] + (1 - alpha) * x[n]
float applyLowPassFilter(float input)
{
    lpfPrevOutput = LPF_ALPHA * lpfPrevOutput + (1.0 - LPF_ALPHA) * input;
    return lpfPrevOutput;
}

// ==========================================
// âœ¨ NEW: Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC NOTCH (Loáº¡i 50Hz/60Hz)
// ==========================================
// IIR Biquad Notch Filter
// Removes power line interference (50Hz EU / 60Hz US)
float applyNotchFilter(float input)
{
    // Direct Form II implementation
    float output = notch_b0 * input + notch_b1 * notch_x1 + notch_b2 * notch_x2 - notch_a1 * notch_y1 - notch_a2 * notch_y2;

    // Shift delays
    notch_x2 = notch_x1;
    notch_x1 = input;
    notch_y2 = notch_y1;
    notch_y1 = output;

    return output;
}

// ==========================================
// Bá»˜ Lá»ŒC HIGH-PASS (Loáº¡i baseline drift)
// ==========================================
float applyHighPassFilter(float input)
{
    float output = HPF_ALPHA * (hpfPrevOutput + input - hpfPrevInput);

    hpfPrevInput = input;
    hpfPrevOutput = output;

    return output;
}

// ==========================================
// KIá»‚M TRA LEAD-OFF
// ==========================================
bool checkLeadsConnected()
{
    bool loPlus = digitalRead(LO_PLUS_PIN);
    bool loMinus = digitalRead(LO_MINUS_PIN);
    return !(loPlus || loMinus);
}

// ==========================================
// Äá»ŒC VÃ€ Lá»ŒC TÃN HIá»†U - IMPROVED PIPELINE
// ==========================================
void readAndFilterECG()
{
    leadsConnected = checkLeadsConnected();

    if (!leadsConnected)
    {
        rawSignal = 0;
        filteredSignal = 0;
        heartRate = 0;
        return;
    }

    // Äá»c ADC
    int16_t adcValue = ads.readADC_SingleEnded(0);
    rawSignal = adcValue * 0.1875; // Convert to mV

    // ðŸ”„ IMPROVED FILTER PIPELINE:
    float step1 = applyMovingAverage(rawSignal);  // Remove HF noise
    float step2 = applyMedianFilter(step1);       // Remove spikes
    float step3 = applyLowPassFilter(step2);      // âœ¨ NEW: Smooth signal (40Hz)
    float step4 = applyNotchFilter(step3);        // âœ¨ NEW: Remove 50Hz/60Hz
    filteredSignal = applyHighPassFilter(step4);  // Remove DC drift

    lastSignalTime = millis();

    if (!buffersInitialized)
    {
        buffersInitialized = (movingAvgIndex == 0 && medianIndex == 0);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// PHÃT HIá»†N NHá»ŠP TIM - IMPROVED
// ==========================================
void detectHeartRate()
{
    if (!leadsConnected || !buffersInitialized)
    {
        heartRate = 0;
        debounceCounter = 0;
        return;
    }

    unsigned long currentTime = millis();

    // Timeout
    if (currentTime - lastSignalTime > SIGNAL_TIMEOUT)
    {
        heartRate = 0;
        peakCount = 0;
        hrWindowStart = currentTime;
        debounceCounter = 0;
        return;
    }

    // Adaptive baseline & peak
    float absSignal = abs(filteredSignal);
    signalBaseline = signalBaseline * 0.98 + absSignal * 0.02; // Slower adapt
    
    if (absSignal > signalPeak)
        signalPeak = absSignal;
    else
        signalPeak *= 0.995; // Slower decay

    // Dynamic threshold
    float threshold = signalBaseline + (signalPeak - signalBaseline) * THRESHOLD_MULTIPLIER;

    // Peak detection with debounce
    if (absSignal > threshold)
    {
        if (currentTime - lastPeakTime > MIN_PEAK_DISTANCE)
        {
            debounceCounter++;
            
            // âœ¨ NEW: Chá»‰ Ä‘áº¿m khi cÃ³ DEBOUNCE_COUNT peaks liÃªn tiáº¿p
            if (debounceCounter >= DEBOUNCE_COUNT)
            {
                peakCount++;
                lastPeakTime = currentTime;
                debounceCounter = 0;

                if (hrWindowStart == 0)
                    hrWindowStart = currentTime;
            }
        }
    }
    else
    {
        // Reset debounce náº¿u signal drop
        if (debounceCounter > 0)
            debounceCounter--;
    }

    // Calculate HR every 5 seconds
    unsigned long windowDuration = currentTime - hrWindowStart;
    if (windowDuration >= 5000 && peakCount > 0)
    {
        heartRate = (peakCount * 60000) / windowDuration;

        // Validate range
        if (heartRate < 40 || heartRate > 200)
            heartRate = 0;

        // Reset
        peakCount = 0;
        hrWindowStart = currentTime;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// Cáº¬P NHáº¬T CHÃNH
// ==========================================
void updateAD8232()
{
    static unsigned long lastSampleTime = 0;
    unsigned long currentTime = millis();
    unsigned long sampleInterval = 1000 / SAMPLE_RATE;

    if (currentTime - lastSampleTime >= sampleInterval)
    {
        lastSampleTime = currentTime;
        readAndFilterECG();
        detectHeartRate();
    }
}

// ==========================================
// GETTERS
// ==========================================
float getECGRawSignal()
{
    return rawSignal;
}

float getECGFilteredSignal()
{
    return filteredSignal;
}

int getHeartRate()
{
    return heartRate;
}

bool areLeadsConnected()
{
    return leadsConnected;
}

    }
}

// ==========================================
// âœ¨ NEW: PERFORMANCE METRICS
// ==========================================
void printPerformanceMetrics()
{
    static unsigned long sampleCount = 0;
    static unsigned long lastMetricTime = 0;
    unsigned long currentTime = millis();

    sampleCount++;

    if (currentTime - lastMetricTime >= 5000)
    {
        float actualSampleRate = (sampleCount * 1000.0) / (currentTime - lastMetricTime);

        Serial.println("\nðŸ“Š PERFORMANCE METRICS:");
        Serial.print("  - Target Sample Rate: ");
        Serial.print(SAMPLE_RATE);
        Serial.println(" Hz");
        Serial.print("  - Actual Sample Rate: ");
        Serial.print(actualSampleRate, 2);
        Serial.println(" Hz");
        Serial.print("  - Samples: ");
        Serial.println(sampleCount);
        Serial.print("  - Buffer Initialized: ");
        Serial.println(buffersInitialized ? "YES" : "NO");
        Serial.println("â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€");

        sampleCount = 0;
        lastMetricTime = currentTime;
    }
}

 * BÆ¯á»šC 1: Thay Ä‘á»•i includes
 * BÆ¯á»šC 2: Khá»Ÿi táº¡o LVGL thay vÃ¬ TFT_eSPI

 */

#include <Arduino.h>

#include <HTTPClient.h>

#include <Adafruit_VL53L0X.h>
